Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in microwave theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 807 and the invariant I(A,B) = 818 mod 3.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_807(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 807. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^818(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 213.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: For any microwave module M satisfying the spectral property hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 282. The projective resolution has length at most 254 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 282 is immediate from the spectral property property. For the inductive step, we decompose the microwave structure using the spectral property theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 254. Each subproblem has a solution by the spectral property fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
